SELECT "c0"."Name", "b"."Id", "b"."Author", "b"."AverageRating", "b"."CategoryId", "b"."ConcurrencyToken", "b"."CreatedAt", "b"."Description", "b"."ImageUrl", "b"."IsDeleted", "b"."IsFeatured", "b"."LowStockThreshold", "b"."PublicationDate", "b"."StockQuantity", "b"."Title", "b"."TotalReviews", "b"."UpdatedAt", "b"."ISBN", "b"."Price", "b"."Currency", "c0"."Id", "c0"."ConcurrencyToken", "c0"."CreatedAt", "c0"."Description", "c0"."IsDeleted", "c0"."UpdatedAt"
FROM "Books" AS "b"
LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT "c"."Id", "c"."ConcurrencyToken", "c"."CreatedAt", "c"."Description", "c"."IsDeleted", "c"."Name", "c"."UpdatedAt"
    FROM "Categories" AS "c"
    WHERE NOT ("c"."IsDeleted")
) AS "c0" ON "b"."CategoryId" = "c0"."Id"
WHERE NOT ("b"."IsDeleted")
ORDER BY "c0"."Name"

LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT "c"."Id", "c"."ConcurrencyToken", "c"."CreatedAt", "c"."Description", "c"."IsDeleted", "c"."Name", "c"."UpdatedAt"
    FROM "Categories" AS "c"
    WHERE NOT ("c"."IsDeleted")
) AS "c0" ON "b"."CategoryId" = "c0"."Id"
WHERE NOT ("b"."IsDeleted")
ORDER BY "c0"."Name"

FROM "Books" AS "b"
LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT "c"."Id", "c"."ConcurrencyToken", "c"."CreatedAt", "c"."Description", "c"."IsDeleted", "c"."Name", "c"."UpdatedAt"
    FROM "Categories" AS "c"
    WHERE NOT ("c"."IsDeleted")
) AS "c0" ON "b"."CategoryId" = "c0"."Id"
WHERE NOT ("b"."IsDeleted")
ORDER BY "c0"."Name"

LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT "c"."Id", "c"."ConcurrencyToken", "c"."CreatedAt", "c"."Description", "c"."IsDeleted", "c"."Name", "c"."UpdatedAt"
    FROM "Categories" AS "c"
    WHERE NOT ("c"."IsDeleted")
) AS "c0" ON "b"."CategoryId" = "c0"."Id"
WHERE NOT ("b"."IsDeleted")
ORDER BY "c0"."Name"

FROM "Books" AS "b"
LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT "c"."Id", "c"."ConcurrencyToken", "c"."CreatedAt", "c"."Description", "c"."IsDeleted", "c"."Name", "c"."UpdatedAt"
    FROM "Categories" AS "c"
    WHERE NOT ("c"."IsDeleted")
) AS "c0" ON "b"."CategoryId" = "c0"."Id"
WHERE NOT ("b"."IsDeleted")
ORDER BY "c0"."Name"
